---
layout: page
title: "Appendix B: every WebKit IPC message"
description: >-
    Every message of every WebKit IPC interface of the macOS Safari 18.5
    source, tag WebKit-7621.2.5.11.8.
permalink: /appendices/f3_b_ipc_messages/
---

This appendix belongs to the post
[Talking through the walls: WebKit IPC and how to find messages]({% post_url 2026-10-17-f3_talking_through_the_walls_webkit_ipc %}).
It lists every message of every WebKit IPC interface in the macOS Safari
18.5 source, tag `WebKit-7621.2.5.11.8`. The list was generated by
`ipc_map.py` [1], which also writes every message with its arguments and
reply.

The directions follow Appendix A [2]. Each one folds out to a line per
interface, and each interface folds out to its file, its notes and a
table of its messages. A **synchronous** message blocks the sender until
the reply arrives, a message **with a reply** gets its reply later and a
**one-way** message gets none. An interface with more than one direction
appears under each of them, but its messages are listed only under its
first one. The notes use the same labels as Appendix A.

<details markdown="1">
<summary><b>WebContent -> UI</b>: 36 interfaces, 780 messages</summary>
